Three-year rolling robustness analysis

Run ID: rolling-3y-20261114T094827Z-827b90bbfb

14 complete and 3 incomplete windows. Only complete windows are included in aggregates and charts; incomplete windows remain separately identified in detail and pairwise outputs.

Each window starts with fresh initial capital. Window end dates are exclusive; the last available close before the end is the final valuation. Indicators may use preceding available history, but returns and portfolio state are restricted to each window. Overlapping windows are dependent, so cross-window standard deviations are descriptive, not independent-sample estimates.

Portfolio, trade, cost, and volatility metric definitions follow METRICS.md. Dataset limitations include survivorship bias because historical index membership is unavailable.
